snātvā śukle vāsasī paridhāya devāgāraṃ praviśya namaskṛtvānnādy abhighārya tatra hute devebhya udvāhāgnau devapatnībhyāṃ hutvā baliṃ haret | kṛte devātithye yathāsvaṃ gṛhīṇas tarpayitvā śeṣaṃ patnī gupte bhuktvā bhāṇḍāni nirṇejya prakṣālya bahir uttarapūrvasyāṃ diśi vāstūpataye bhūpataye rudrāya nama iti ninīyaivaṃ sāyaṃ śṛtādi yathārthasaṃviṣṭeṣu namo bhuvanapate rudrāya bhasmasade bhasmanā rakṣāṃ karomīti bhasmanā dvāram apidhāya svāmīsutādīn ātmānaṃ cālayet | yac cānyad rakṣyaṃ syāt tat bhasmanā spṛśed ity arthaḥ | ānamya bhartṛpādau saṃviśen nopaviṣṭā sthita āsīta noccair na vitarkasthāne nāvīkṣaṇam īkṣyet | parimṛjya saṃvāhayitvopāsīta vyajanenoṣṇe varṣagharmāṃbho ‘sya gātrebhyaḥ parimṛjyāt | grāmāntargataṃ bhārākrāntaṃ śayānam udyacched dhṛṣṭamanā arthenārcayet | tathā paraśayanāsanavastrābharaṇāni manasāpi nādhyavasyed iti | tathaikapātryaṃ madyamāṃsocchiṣṭanirmālyaṃ cānyatra gurubhartṛsutebhyaḥ iti |
skānde |
tīrthasnānārthinī nārī patipādodakaṃ pibet |
śaṅkarād api viṣṇor vā patir eko ‘dhikaḥ striyāḥ ||
paraśurāmapratāpe[88] ratnāvalyāṃ ca skānde |
prātaḥkāle tu yā nārī dadyād arghyaṃ vivasvate |
saptajanmasu[89] vaidhavyaṃ sā nārī naiva paśyati ||
kṛtvā tu maṇḍalaṃ bāhye sūryaṃ tatrākṣatādibhiḥ |
pūjayet satataṃ yā vai tasyās tuṣyanti devatāḥ ||
prātaḥkāle tu yā nārī śucir bhūtvā samāhitā |
pūjayed dvāradeśaṃ tu sarvān kāmān samaśnute ||
na dadāti yadā nārī jyeṣṭhāyai pratyahaṃ balim |
bhojyāc caiva yathāśakti sā pretya narakaṃ vrajet ||
avaśyam eva nārībhir jyeṣṭhāyā balikarmaṇā |
prīṇanaṃ pratyahaṃ kāryaṃ putrapautradhanepsubhiḥ ||
kalpatarau bhārate |
yadaiva bhartā jānīyān mantramūlaparāṃ striyam |
udvijeta tadaivāsyāḥ sarpād veśmagatād iva ||
tatraiva viṣṇūktaṃ [244] pṛthvīṃ prati śrīvākyam |
nārīṣu nityaṃ śucibhūtāsu
svabhartṛbhaktapriyavādinīṣu |
amuktahastāsu sutānvitāsu
suguptabhāṇḍāsu balipriyāsu ||
sumṛṣṭaveśmāsu jitendriyāsu
kalivyapetāsu vilolupāsu |
dharmavyapekṣāsu dayānvitāsu
sthitā sadāhaṃ jagatāṃ vidhātrī ||
vyāsaḥ |
dvāropaveśanaṃ nityaṃ gavākṣeṇa nirīkṣaṇam |
asatpralāpo hāsyaṃ ca dūṣaṇaṃ kulayoṣitām ||
bhārate |
aṭṭaśūlā janapadāḥ śivaśūlāś catuṣpathāḥ |
vanitāḥ keśaśūlinyo bhaviṣyanti kalau yuge ||
asyārtham āha paraśurāmaḥ |
aṭṭam annaṃ śivo vedaḥ śūlaṃ vikraya eva ca |
keśaś ca bhagam ity āhur vedatattvārthadarśinaḥ ||
manuḥ |
bhartā devo[90] gurur bhartā dharmatīrthavratāni ca |
tasmāt sarvaṃ parityajya patim ekaṃ samarcayet ||
bhukte bhuṅkte patau yā tu svāsīnā vāpi cāsite[91] |
vinidrite vinidrāti sā strī jñeyā pativratā ||
aṅgirāḥ |
ārtārte mudite hṛṣṭā proṣite malinā kṛśā |
mṛte mriyeta yā patyau sā strī jñeyā pativratā ||
rajovatīgarbhiṇīdharmāḥ sahagamanānvārohaṇādau nirṇayo vidhiś ca nirṇayasindhāv ukto ‘smābhiḥ |
atha vidhavādharmāḥ |
madanaratna-pārijātayoḥ skānde |
patyau mṛte ‘pi yā yoṣid vaidhavyaṃ pālayet kvacit |
sā punaḥ prāpya bhartāraṃ svargalokaṃ samaśnute ||
vidhavākabarībandho bhartṛbandhāya jāyate |
śiraso vapanaṃ tasmāt kāryaṃ vidhavayā sadā ||
ekāhāraḥ sadā kāryo no dvitīyaḥ kadācana |
trirātraṃ pañcarātraṃ vā pakṣavratam athāpi vā ||
māsopavāsaṃ vā kuryāc cāndrāyaṇam athāpi vā |
kṛcchraṃ parākaṃ vā kuryāt taptakṛcchram athāpi vā ||
yavānnena phalāhāraiḥ śākāhāraiḥ payovrataiḥ |
prāṇayātrāṃ prakurvīta yāvat prāṇaḥ svayaṃ vrajet ||
paryaṅkaśāyinī nārī vidhavā pātayet patim |
tasmād bhūśayanaṃ kāryaṃ patisaukhyasamīhayā ||
naivāṅgodvartanaṃ kāryaṃ striyā vidhavayā kvacit |
gandhadravyasy saṃyogo naiva kāryaḥ svayaṃ punaḥ ||
tarpaṇaṃ pratyahaṃ kāryaṃ bhartuḥ kuśatilodakaiḥ |
tat pituś cāpi kartavyaṃ nāmagotrādipūrvakam ||
idaṃ putrādyabhāve |
viṣṇos tu pūjanaṃ kāryaṃ patibudhyā na cānyathā |
patim eva sadā dhyāyed viṣṇurūpadharaṃ param ||
yad yad iṣṭatamaṃ loke yad yat pūrvaṃ samīhitam |
tat tad guṇavate deyaṃ patiprīṇanakāmyayā ||
vaiśākhe kārttike māghe viśeṣaniyamaṃ caret |
snānaṃ dānaṃ tīrthayātrāṃ viṣṇor nāmagrahaṃ muhuḥ ||
vaiśākhe jalakumbhāś ca kārttike ghṛtadīpakāḥ |
māghe tiladhanotsargāt svargaloke viśiṣyate ||
prapā kāryā ca vaiśākhe deve deyā galantikā |
upānad vyajanaṃ chatraṃ sūkṣmavāsāṃsi candanam ||
deyāni dvijamukhyebhyaḥ patir me prīyatām iti |
evaṃvidhaiś ca vidhavā vividhiar niyamair vrataiḥ ||
vaiśākhān kārttikān māghān evam evātivāhayet |
nādhirohed anaḍvāhaṃ prāṇaiḥ kaṇṭhagatair api ||
kañcukaṃ na parīdadhyād vāso na vikṛtaṃ vaset |
evaṃ dharmasamāyuktā vidhavāpi pativratā ||
patilokam avāpnoti na bhavet kvāpi duḥkhinī |
śrāddhaṃ ca ṣaṭpiṇḍaṃ kāryaṃ,
svabhartṛprabhṛtitribhyaḥ svapitṛbhyas tathaiva ca |
vidhavā kārayec chrāddhaṃ yathākālam atandritā ||
iti smṛtiratnāvalyāṃ vṛddhamanūkteḥ |
suvratānāṃ tāsāṃ tu lokāḥ patibhiḥ
samānāḥ gomāyulokā vyabhicāriṇīnām |
hārīta |
pativratā tu sādhvī [251] yā niṣṭhāṃ yāti prabhau mṛte |
sā hitvā sarvapāpāni patilokam avāpnuyāt ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
vratopavāsaniratā brahmacaryavyavasthitā |
damadānaratā nityam aputrāpi divaṃ vrajet ||
manuḥ |
kāmaṃ tu kṣapayed dehaṃ puṣpamūlaphalodakaiḥ |
na tu nāmāpi gṛhṇīyāt patyau prete parasya tu ||
manu-yamau |
āsītāmaraṇāt kṣāntā niyatā brahmacāriṇī |
dharmeṇaiva ca[92] kāṅkṣanti satpatitvam anuttamam ||
striyāḥ śrutau vā śāstre vā pravrajyā nābhidhīyate |
prajā hi tasyāḥ svo dharmaḥ savarṇād iti dhāraṇā ||
aṣṭāśītisahasrāṇi munīnām ūrdhvaretasām |
divaṃ gatāni viprāṇām akṛtvā kulasaṃtatim ||
mṛte bhartari yā nārī brahmacaryavyavasthitā[93] |
svargaṃ gacchaty aputrāpi yathā te brahmacāriṇaḥ ||
skānde |
apṛṭvā tu sutān kiṃcin na kuryād bhartṛtatparā ||
iti vidhavādharmāḥ |
[253]
atha śūdrasamānadharmāḥ |
tathā kuṇḍagoladāv api śūdrasamau | tathā ca smṛtikaumudyāṃ devalaḥ |
dvitīyena tu yaḥ [254] pitrā savarṇāyāṃ prajāyate |
savarṇōḍha iti jñeyaḥ śūdradharmā ca jātitaḥ ||
vratahīnās tv asaṃskāryāḥ savarṇāsv api ye sutāḥ |
utpāditāḥ sarvarṇena vrātyā iti bahiṣkṛtāḥ || iti |
tatraiva manuḥ |
paradāreṣu jāyete dvau sutau kuṇḍagolakau |
amṛte jārajaḥ kuṇḍo mṛte bhartar golakaḥ ||
nanu kuṇḍagolakayor abrahmaṇatve kathaṃ śraddhe niṣedhaḥ | sa hi brāhmaṇaḥ śrāddhasaṃpade ity ukter brāhmaṇamātraprāptau teṣāṃ madhye keṣāṃcin niṣedhārthaḥ | yad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
rogī hīnātiriktāṅgaḥ kāṇaḥ paurnarbhavas tathā |
avakīrṇī kuṇḍagolau kunakhī śyāvadantakaḥ ||
iti, tayoś cābrāhmaṇye prāptyabhāvān niṣedho vyarthaḥ | tathā smṛtyarthasāre brāhme ca tayoḥ saṃskāryatvam uktam | kecid āhur dvijair jātau saṃskāryau kuṇḍagolakāv iti | tad api na yujyate iti |
ucyate | “brāhmaṇyāṃ brāhmaṇāj jāto brāhmaṇaḥ” iti bhramaprāptasya śrāddhe niṣedha iti vijñāneśvaraḥ (on YDh 1.90) | kuṇḍagolakayoḥ saṃskāryatvaṃ śrāddhe niṣedhaś ca kṣetrajaparaḥ, śāstrānujñānāt | anyasyābrāhmaṇatvena prāptyabhāvād upādheyasaṃkare ‘py upādher ā saṃkarād ity aparārkaḥ | kalpatarau kātyāyanaḥ |
sukhārthaṃ yā pravṛttā strī na bhartuḥ sutakāmyayā |
putraṃ tu janayed evaṃ nindyā pāpā ca sā smṛtā ||
bhartṛgotraṃ samutsṛjya nārī yady anyam āśrayet |
nindyaiva sā smṛtā loke parapūrveti cocyate ||
parapūrvā tu yatra syāt tatra syān nāśritaḥ prabhuḥ |
kramād ṛte tu dharmārthaṃ labdhvā vā svāmino bhavet ||
niroddhavyā ca tāḍyā ca parapūrvāparādhataḥ |
caurahastān nadīvegād durbhikṣād deśaviplavāt ||
vinistīrya sakāntārāl labdhā tyaktā kramāgatā |
āsāṃ bhoge na doṣaḥ syād iti śāstraviniścayaḥ ||
svāmidattā tu gṛhṇīyād deyā cen na tv ato ‘nyathā |
dīyamānam athātmānaṃ yā nārī nānumodate |
na sā deyā na ca grāhyā vidhir eṣa smṛto budhaiḥ ||
na cottamāṃ na cākāmāṃ tathā putravatīṃ na ca |
īdṛśīṃ tv anurūpeṇa niṣkrayeṇa pramīlayet ||
yamaḥ |
jārajātaḥ savarṇāyāṃ kuṇḍo jīvati bhartari |
mṛte tu golako nāma jātihīnau ca tau smṛtau ||
asavarṇāsu nārīṣu dvijair utpāditāś ca ye |
parapatnīṣu sarvāsu kuṇḍās te golakāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
mātṛvarṇā na te proktāḥ pitṛvarṇā na ca smṛtāḥ |
avivāhyāḥ sutāś caiṣāṃ bandhubhiḥ pitṛmātṛtaḥ ||
sa eva |
kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ kuryād dhatvā saṃkīrṇayonijam |
sūtaṃ hatvātikṛcchraṃ tu kuṇḍaṃ golakam eva ca ||
manuḥ |
jāto nāryām anāryāyām āryād āryo bhaved guṇaiḥ |
jāto ‘py anāryād āryāyām anārya iti niścayaḥ ||
nīcāyām uttamajau saṃskāryau, uttamāyāṃ nīcajau nety arthaḥ | smṛtikaumudyām ādipurāṇe |
savarṇāsu savarṇais tu ye jāyante parair api |
avaroṭakasaṃjṅās te vratasaṃskāravarjitāḥ ||
viprāyāṃ viprajā upaneyā nānye iti tatraivoktam |
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ jīvatām anyasaṃbhavaḥ |
kuṇḍas tu saṃkaro jñeyo mṛtānām atha golakaḥ ||
jātihīnaḥ sa mātṝṇāṃ grāhayet karmanāmanī |
tadīyaṃ karma nāma ca tatmātṝṇām ity arthaḥ |
yojyo devapure rājñā varṇasaṃkarabhīruṇā |
devapure devadvāre |
kuṇḍo vā golako vipraḥ saṃdhyopāsanamātravit ||
snānabhojanasaṃdhyāsu deveṣu sa paṭhec ca tat |
vipra ity uktes tasyaiva saṃdhyopanayanaṃ ca | [255] mitākṣarāyām api kuṇdgolakau yathārhaṃ saṃskāryāv ity uktam | smṛtyarthasāre prayogapārijāte brāhme ca |
evam eva dvijair jātau saṃskāryau kuṇḍagolakau | iti |
snāneti snānādisamaye bandivad vākyaṃ paṭhed ity arthaḥ |
iti kuṇḍagolakau |
[257]
atha klībadharmāḥ |
tathā dvijotpannaḥ ṣaṇḍho ‘pi śūdrasamaḥ | “vasante brāhmaṇam upanayīta” ity upanayanavidhau camāsādhvaryuvad upādeyatvena vivakṣitapuṃstvāt strīṇām iva tasyāpy upanayanābhāvāt | aṣṭavarṣavākye uddeśyatve ‘pi prāptānuvādena puṃsa evānuvādāt | mitākṣarāyāṃ devalo ‘pi |
ṣaṇḍhako liṅgahīnaḥ syāt saṃskārārhaś ca naiva saḥ | iti |
ata eva tatraiva vṛddhamanuḥ |
ṣaṇḍhaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ hatvā śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret | iti |
baudhāyanasūtra smṛtyarthasāre ca vacanād upanayanam uktam | prayogapārijāte brāhme ‘pi |
brāhmaṇyāṃ brāhmaṇāj jāto brāhmaṇaḥ iti śrutiḥ |
tasmāc ca ṣaṇḍhabadhirakubjavāmanapaṅguṣu ||
ityādy uktvā,
dhvastapuṃstveṣu caiteṣu saṃskārāḥ syur yathocitym | iti |
etac coktaṃ prāk | sa ca saptavidhaḥ | tathā ca gauḍadhṛtadevalaḥ |
paṇḍhako vātajaḥ paṇḍhaḥ paṇḍaḥ klībo napuṃsakaṃ |
kīlakaś cety saptaiva klībabhedo vyavasthitaḥ ||
eṣāṃ svarūpam āha sa eva |
teṣāṃ strītulyavākceṣṭaḥ strītulyaḥ paṇḍhako bhavet |
unnatakucakakūrpahīnatvādidharmaka ity arthaḥ |
pumān kṛtvā saliṅgāni paścād bhindyāt tathaiva ca |
strī ca puṃbhāvam āsthāya puruṣācāravadguṇaḥ ||
vātako nāma paṇḍhaḥ syāt strīṣpaṇḍho vāpi nāmataḥ ||
liṅgāni strīpuṃnapuṃsakāni bhindyāt tyajet |
kadācit strī kadācit pumān kadācit paṇḍha ity arthaḥ | strī ca puruṣavat stryantaraṃ bhuṅkte śukrotsargaṃ vinaiva vātavaśād ity arthaḥ |
asalliṅgo ‘tha paṇḍhaḥ syāt paṇḍhas tu mlānamehanaḥ |
amedhyāśī pumān klībo naṣṭaretā napuṃsakaḥ ||
amedhyāśī mukhamaithunāt paraśukrāśī | ayaṃ vaidyake āsekya ity uktaḥ | tad uktaṃ śuśrute |
āsekyo nāma bhavati dampatyor alpavīryayoḥ |
sa śukraṃ prāśya labhate liṅgocchrāyam asaṃśayam ||
sa kīlaka ity prokto yaḥ klaibyād ātamaḥ śriyam |
anyena saha saṃyojya paścāt tām eva sevate ||
suśrute tv anye pi |
yaḥ pūtiyonau jāyeta sa saugandhikasaṃjñakaḥ |
sa yonisephasaugandham āghrāya labhate balam ||
sve gude maithunād yas tu [258] strīṣu puṃvat pravartate |
kumbhīkaḥ sa tu vijñeya īrṣyakaṃ śṛṇu cāparam ||
dṛṣṭvā vyavāyam anyeṣāṃ vyavāye yaḥ pravartate |
īrṣyakaḥ sa tu vijñeyaḥ paṇḍhakaṃ śṛṇu pañcamam ||
yo bhāryāyām ṛtau mohād aṅganeva pravartate |
tataḥ strīceṣṭācāro jāyate paṇḍhasaṃjñitaḥ ||
mādhavīye tu caturdaśa bhedā uktāḥ | te tatraiva jñeyāḥ | teṣāṃ copanayanavratacatuṣṭayasamāvartanavivāhavarjaṃ sarve saṃskārās tūṣṇīṃ bhavanti, “kumāraṃ jātam” ityādau saṃskāryarūpoddeśyagatatvena puṃstvasyāvivakṣayā strīṇām iva paṇḍhasyāpi prāpteḥ,
tūṣṇīm etāḥ kriyāḥ strīṇāṃ vivāhas tu samantrakaḥ |
iti yājñavalkyokteḥ,
te ca strītulyavākceṣṭāḥ strīdharmāṇaḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
iti vṛddhamanūkteś ca | jalāśayāgamaprāsādadevapratiṣṭhāmahādānādiṣv apy evam, ṣāṣṭhanyāyena kāmaśrutigatasya puṃstvasyoddeśyagatatvenāvivakṣayā strīṇām ivādhikārāt | evaṃ sarvadāneṣu, ahiṃsādeḥ sādhāraṇyāc ca | pituḥ śrāddhādau tu nādhikāraḥ | “yaś cārthaharaḥ sapiṇḍadāyī” ity āpastambenārthaharasyaiva piṇḍadānokteḥ, ṣaṇḍhasya ca,
anaṃśau klībapatitau paṅgur unmattako jaḍaḥ |
iti manvādyair arthaharatvaniṣedhāt | yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
klībādyā nodakaṃ kuryus tenāvrātyā vidharminaḥ |
iti ṣaṇḍhāḥ ||
[260]
anye ‘pi śūdrasamāḥ |
kalpatarau yamenoktāḥ |
pravrajyopanivṛttāś ca vṛthāpravrajitāś ca ye |
yaś ca pravaritāj jātaḥ pravrajyāvasitaś ca yaḥ ||
ete śūdradharmāṇo manunā parikīrtitāḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
pravrajyāvasito rājño dāsa āmaraṇāntikaḥ |
anye ‘pi śūdrasamā yājñavalkyenoktāḥ |
sadyaḥ patati māṃsena lākṣayā lavaṇena ca |
māsena śūdro bhavati brāhmaṇaḥ kṣīravikrayāt ||
atra dharmanindāmātram iti vārtike bhaṭṭācāryāḥ | dvijātikarmahāniparāṇīti vijñāneśvaraḥ | atas te śūdrasamāḥ |
atha vrātyāḥ |
tathā vrātyā api | tathā ca manuḥ |
brāhmaṇā kṣatriyā vaiśyā svāvadher ūrdhvam abdataḥ |
akṛtopanayāḥ sarve vṛṣalā eva te smṛtāḥ ||
vrātyastomaṃ vinā | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
ā ṣoḍaśād ā viṃśāc ca caturviṃśāc ca vatsarāt |
brahmakṣatraviśāṃ kāla aupanāyanikaḥ paraḥ ||
ata ūrdhvaṃ patanty ete sarvadharmabahiṣkṛtāḥ |
sāvitrīpatitā vrātyā vrātyastomād ṛte kratoḥ ||
[261]
atha niyogaḥ[94] |
kalpatarau devalaḥ |
naṣṭaḥ pravrajitaḥ klībaḥ patito rājakilbiṣī |
lokāntaragato vāpi parityājyaḥ striyā patiḥ ||
mṛte bhartari jīve vā strī vindetāparaṃ patim |
saṃtatyanāśārthatayā na svātantryeṇa yoṣitaḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
aṣṭau varṣāṇy udīkṣeta brāhmaṇī proṣitaṃ patim |
aprasūtā tu catvāri dve same ca prasūtikā ||
na śūdrāyāḥ smṛtaḥ kālo na ca dharmavyatikramaḥ |
viśeṣato prasūtāyāḥ striyāḥ saṃvatsarā sthitiḥ ||
apravṛttau smṛtaḥ kāla eṣaḥ śeṣitayoṣitāṃ |
jīvati śrūyamāṇe tu syād eṣa dviguṇo ‘vadhiḥ ||
prajāpravṛttau nārīṇāṃ vṛttir eṣā prajāpateḥ |
ato ‘nyagamane strīṇām eṣa doṣo na vidyate ||
iti niyogaḥ
atha śūdrasamān vaktuṃ saṃkarajā ucyante |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
viprān mūrdhāvasikto hi kṣatriyāyāṃ viśaḥ striyāṃ |
ambaṣṭhaḥ śūdryām [262] niṣādo jātaḥ pārasavo ‘pi vā ||
vaiśyāśudayos tu rājanyān māhiṣyograu sutau smṛtau |
vaiśyāt tu karaṇaḥ śūdryāṃ vinnāsv eṣa vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||
evaṃ mūrdhāvaśikāmbaṣṭhapārasavamāhiṣyograkaraṇāḥ ṣaḍ anulomāḥ, mātṛjātyuktakarmabhājaś ca | ete hīnavarṇāsu jātāḥ | etāsu cotkṛṣṭajā anulomāḥ | nīcajāḥ pratilomāḥ | manuḥ |
brāhmaṇād ugrakanyāyām āvṛto nāma jāyate |
śūdrāyāṃ kṣatrajā ugrāḥ | yājñavalkyaḥ |
māhiṣyeṇa karaṇyāṃ tu rathakāraḥ prajāyate |
kṣatrād vaiśyāyāṃ māhiṣyaḥ | vaiśyāc chūdrāyāṃ karaṇī tasyāṃ rathakāraḥ | eṣāṃ vṛttīr āha smṛtikaumudyāṃ vasiṣṭhaḥ |
mūrdhāvasiktānāṃ hastyaśvarathaśastraśikṣāstradhāraṇaṃ ca | maṇimantrauṣadhaiḥ prāṇirakṣaṇam ambaṣṭhānām | māhiṣyāṇāṃ gītanṛtyanakṣatrair jīvanaṃ sasyarakṣā ca | pārasavograkaraṇānāṃ dvijaśuśrūṣā dhanādhyakṣatā rājasevā durgaṇtaḥpurarakṣā ca | rathakārasya nidhijñānaṃ hastyaśvasārathyavardhanapoṣaṇadamanādi |
śaṅkhaḥ |
kṣatriyavaiśyānulomānantarotpannajo rathakāras tasyādhyayanejyādānopanayana-saṃskārāś cāpratiṣiddhāḥ | rathasūtravāstuvidyādhyayanavṛttitā ceti |
jātiviveke |
śūdrāṃ śayanam āropya brāhmaṇaś cet kathaṃcana |
janayed grāmyadharmeṇa tasyāṃ pārasavaṃ sutam ||
mahāśūdraḥ sa vikhyātaḥ śūdrebhyaḥ kiṃcid uttamaḥ |
svarṇakārasya tasyeha snānaṃ dhautraṃ pavitrakam ||
sarvaṃ śūdrasya dharmeṇa vartanaṃ tasya jīvikā |
tathā |
kūṭasvarṇavyavahārī vimāṃsasya ca vikrayī |
rājñā vipras tu kartavyo dāpyaś cottamasāhasam ||
uśanāḥ |
kṣatriyāviprasaṃyogāj jāto mūrdhāvasiktakaḥ |
rājanyaḥ kṣatradharmebhyo ‘bhyadhikaḥ saṃprakīrtitaḥ ||
ātharvaṇakriyāṃ kurvan nityanaimittikīṃ kriyām |
aśvaṃ rathaṃ hastinaṃ vā vāhayed vai nṛpājñayā ||
senāpatyaṃ ca bhaiṣajyaṃ kuryāc jivec ca vṛttiṣu |
āyurvedam athāṣṭāṅgaṃ tatroktaṃ dharmataś caret ||
jyotiṣaṃ gaṇitaṃ vāpi kāyikīṃ vṛttim ācaret |
vaiśyāyāṃ vidhinā viprāj jāto hy ambaṣṭha ucyate ||
kṛṣyājīvo bhavet tasya tathaivāgre pranartakaḥ |
naravājigajādīnāṃ cikitsā tasya jīvikā ||
vāgbhaṭṭaḥ |
sapta vā pañca vā tisro yo na vetti hi saṃhitāḥ |
rājñā tasya kuvaidyasya chettavyo dakṣiṇaḥ karaḥ ||
manuḥ |
prāyaścittaṃ cikitsāṃ ca jyotiṣaṃ dharmanirṇayam |
vinā śāstreṇa yo brūyāt tam āhur brahmaghātakam ||
ayaṃ śastravaidyaḥ |
vaiśyāyāṃ kṣatriyāj jāto māhiṣyas tv anulomajaḥ |
aṣṭādhikāranirataś catuḥṣaṣṭyaṅgakovidaḥ ||
vrabandhādikās tasya kriyāḥ syuḥ sakalā viśaḥ |
jyotiṣaṃ śākunaṃ śāstraṃ svaraśāstraṃ jīvikā ||
viśvaṃbharaśāstre |
śūdryāṃ jātas tu vaiśyād vai karaṇo lipilekhakṛt |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
jātyutkarṣo yuge jñeyaḥ saptame pañcame ‘pi vā |
vyatyaye karmaṇāṃ sāmyaṃ pūrvavac cādharottaram ||
jātyutkarṣa utkṛṣṭajātiprāptiḥ saptame | apaśabdāt ṣaṣṭhe pañcame vā jñeyaḥ | ayam arthaḥ | viproḍhā śūdrā kanyāṃ sūte | sāpi tadūḍhānyām iti saptamaṃ vipraṃ [263] sūte | evaṃ viproḍhavaiśyājanyā kanyāṃ ṣaṣthaṃ vipram | viproḍhakṣatriyājanyā pañcamam | kṣatriyāc chūdrotpannā ṣaṣṭhaṃ kṣatriyam | vaiśyajā pañcamaṃ vaiśyam iti pūrvārdhārthaḥ | evaṃ viprasya kṣātravṛttyāpatyaparaṃparayā jīvane pañcamaḥ kṣatriyaḥ | vaiśyavṛttau jīvane ṣaṣṭho vaiśyaḥ | śūdravṛttau saptamaḥ śūdraḥ | kṣatrasya vaiśyavṛttau pañcamo vaiśyaḥ | śūdravṛttau ṣaṣṭhaḥ śūdraḥ | vaiśyasya sūdravṛttau pañcamaḥ śudra iti |
[265]
atha pratilomajāḥ |
kalpatarau kātyāyanaḥ |
niyogotpādanaṃ kuryād yathoktaṃ tadviśuddhaye |
dvijastrīṣu ca dharmo ‘yaṃ śūdrasyaitat sadā bhavet ||
śūdreṣu na niyoga ityarthaḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ |
ukto niyogo manunā niṣiddhaḥ svayam eva tu |
yugahrāsād aśakto ‘yaṃ kartuṃ martyair vidhānataḥ ||
anekadhā kṛtāḥ putrā ṛṣibhir ye purātanaiḥ |
na śakyante ‘dhunā kartuṃ śaktihīnair idaṃtanaiḥ ||
tatraiva hārītaḥ |
svairiṇī ca punarbhūś ca retodhāḥ kāmacāriṇī |
sarvabhakṣā ca vijñeyāḥ pañcaitāḥ śūdrayonayaḥ ||
etāsāṃ yāny apatyāni utpadyante kadācana |
na tān paṅktiṣu yuñjīta na te paṅktyarhakāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
retodhāḥ kuṇḍamātā | kāmacāriṇī caturbhyo ‘dhikapuruṣagā | sarvabhakṣā surāpī |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
brāhmaṇyāṃ kṣatriyāt sūto vaiśyād vaidehakas tathā |
śūdrāj jātas tu cāṇḍālaḥ sarvadharmabahiṣkṛtaḥ ||
kṣatriyā māgadhaṃ vaiśyāc chūdrāt kṣattāram eva ca |
śūdrād āyogavaṃ vaiśyā janayāmāsa vai sutam ||
māgadho bandī | kṣattā niṣādo bhilla iti prasiddhaḥ | āyogavaḥ pulkasaḥ | tad āha hārītaḥ |
kṣatriyāyāṃ vaiśyaśūdrau bandiniṣādau janayato vaiśyāyāṃ śūdraḥ pulkasān iti |
vyāso ‘pi |
śūdrān niṣādo matsyaghnaḥ kṣatriyāyāṃ vyatikramāt |
tena dvau niṣādau | ekaḥ prāg ukto ‘nulomaḥ | ayaṃ caikaḥ | jātiviveke |
kṣatriyā śūdrasaṃyogāt kṣattāraṃ janayet sutam |
niṣāda iti vikhyātaḥ sarvavarṇabahiṣkṛtaḥ ||
śūdrācāravihīnaś ca pāpadhīnirataḥ sadā |
vāgurāpāśapāṇis tu mṛgabandhanakovidaḥ ||
āraṇyapaśujātīnām antakaś ca vanecaraḥ |
krodhānvito māṃsavṛttyā tayā jīvet sadaiva hi ||
ghaṇṭāṃ pāparddhikāṃ kuryād rātrau vismayakāriṇīm |
dvidhā ca mṛgayā proktā vyomabhūcāriṇām iha ||
vikrayaṃ madhunaḥ kryād dhanam icchan samṛddhaye |
yamas tu māgadhaniṣādayoḥ saṃjñāntaram āha |
kṣatriyāyāṃ tu vaiśyena mucupo nāma jāyate |
śūdreṇa jāyate madguḥ kṣatriyā yatra muhyati ||
moho maithunam | smṛtyantare |
kṣatriyā māgadhaṃ vaiśyāj janayāmāsa vai sutam |
sa bandījana ity ukto vratabandhavivarjitaḥ ||
śūdrebhyo ‘bhyadhikaḥ kiṃcit tasya jīvanam ucyate |
kāvyālaṃkāragadyādiṣaṭbhāṣāsu kalākṣamaḥ ||
gadyapadyāni citrāṇi viruddhāni mahībhṛtām ||
[266]        kṣatriyāyāṃ vaiśyajātaḥ kṣattā vetradharo bhavet |
laghujātiviveke |
sūtās te ye purāṇasthān uccaranti nṛpān sadā |
ye ‘lpakālagatān śūrān varṇayanti ca māgadhāḥ ||
vartamānān nṛpān samyak kṛtasaṃgrāmakarmaṇaḥ |
varṇayanti prabandhair ye bandhinas te prakīrtitāḥ ||
manuḥ |
kārāvaraṃ niṣādāt tu carmakāraṃ prasūyate |
vaidehīti śeṣaḥ |
vaidehakād andhramedau bahirgrāmapratiśrayau ||
viprāyāṃ vaiśyajo vaidehaḥ | tasmāt kārāvaryām andhraḥ | niṣādyāṃ meda iti medhātithiḥ | aṅgirasā tv anye ‘pi caṇḍālā uktāḥ |
yas tu pravratijā jāto brāhmaṇyāṃ yaś ca śūdrajaḥ |
sagotroḍhāsutaś caiva caṇḍālās traya īritāḥ ||
manuḥ pulkasam apy āha |
jāto niṣādāc chūdrāyāṃ jātyā bhavati pulkasaḥ |
śūdrāj jāto niṣādyāṃ tu sa vai kukkuṭakaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
kṣattur jātas tathogryāṃ tu śvapāka iti cocyate |
vaidehakena tv ambaṣṭhyām utpanno vena ucyate ||
śūdrāt kṣatryājaḥ kṣattā | kṣatrāc chūdrāyām ugraḥ | tābhyāṃ śvapākaḥ | vaiśyād viprāyāṃ vaidehaḥ | viprād vaiśyāyām ambaṣṭhī | tasyāṃ venaḥ | eṣāṃ vṛttīr āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
sūtānāṃ mantrikāyasthatvastrījīvanavastrapūraṇāni | mantritvaṃ kāyastho lekhakas tattvaṃ ca | vaidehakānāṃ naṭavṛttiraṅgāvataraṇe | venānāṃ dārucchedanaṃ bhramaropaṇaṃ maṇimuktāpravālavedhanaṃ ca | bhramaḥ śāṇaḥ | āyogavānāṃ tāmrāyastrapusīsāyudhakaraṇaṃ yojanaṃ nagarakaraṇaṃ ca sairandhrāṇāṃ surāmadyasīdhūkaraṇaṃ tadvikrayaḥ puṣpaphalaśākavikrayaś ca |
viśvaṃbharaśāstre |
viprāyāṃ yaḥ sutaḥ kṣatrāt sūtākhyo gajabandhakṛt|
damanaṃ turagāṇāṃ ca kuryāt tajjīvanaṃ param ||
nṛpād brāhmaṇakanyāyāṃ vivāheṣu samanvayāt |
jātaḥ sūtas tu nirdiṣṭaḥ pratilomavidhir dvijaḥ ||
vivāhasaṃbandhe ūḍhāyāṃ brāhmaṇakanyāyām ity arthaḥ |
vedānarhas tathā caiṣāṃ dharmāṇām anubodhakaḥ |
brāhmaṇyāṃ tu suto jāto vaiśyād vaidehakaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
pāṣāṇakāṣṭayoḥ śilpaṃ tasya jīvanam uttamam ||
manuḥ |
sūtānām aśvasārathyam ambaṣṭhānāṃ cikitsitam |
vaidehakānāṃ strīkāryaṃ māgadhānāṃ vaṇikpathaḥ ||
strīkārym antapurarakṣā | vaṇikpatho rājamārgas tadrakṣā |
matsyaghāto niṣādānāṃ tvaṣṭir āyogavasya ca |
tvaṣṭir dārutakṣaṇaṃ vardhakir ity arthaḥ | uśanasā tu |
nṛpāyāṃ vaiśyasaṃsargād āyogava iti smṛtaḥ |
tantuvāyā bhavanty ete vasukāṃsyopajīvinaḥ ||
śīlikāḥ kecid atraiva jīvanaṃ vastranirmitam |
kṣatrugrapulkasānāṃ tu bilaukovadhabandhane ||
bilaukasaḥ sarpādayaḥ | ugro ‘tra pratilomajaḥ | tanmadhyapāṭhād iti smṛtimañjaryām |
digvaṇānāṃ carmakāryaṃ venānāṃ bhāṇḍavādanam |
bhāṇḍaṃ murajapaṭahādi |
caityadrumaśmaśāneṣu śaileṣūpavaneṣu ca ||
vaseyur ete vijñātā vartayantaḥ svakarmabhiḥ |
vijñātā jñāticihnāṅkitāḥ |
cāṇḍālaśvapacānāṃ tu nirgrāmaḥ syāt pratiśrayaḥ ||
avapātrāś ca kartavyā dhanam eṣāṃ śvagardabham |
nirgrāmo bahirgrāmaḥ | avapātraṃ bhinnapātram | yad vā pākapātrādihīnāḥ | yad vakṣyati “annam eṣāṃ parādhīnam” iti | yad vā taddhastasthe pātre ‘nnādi na deyam, kiṃ tu bhūmisthe |
vāsāṃsi mrtacailāni bhinnabhāṇḍe ca bhojanam ||
kārṣṇāyasam [267] alaṃkāraḥ parivrajyā ca nityadā |
parivrajyā ekatrānavasthānam |
na taiḥ samayam anvicchet puruṣo dharmam ācaran ||
vyvahāro mithas teṣāṃ vivāhaḥ sadṛśaiḥ saha |
annam eṣāṃ parādhīnaṃ deyaṃ syād bhinnabhājane ||
rātrau na vicareyus te grāmeṣu nagareṣu ca |
kāryārthaṃ vicareyur vā cihnatā rājaśāsanaiḥ ||
abāndhavaṃ śavaṃ caiva nirhareyur iti sthitiḥ |
vadhyāṃś ca hanyuḥ satataṃ yathāśāstraṃ nṛpājñayā ||
vadhyavāsāṃsi gṛhṇīyuḥ śayyāś cābharaṇāni ca |
brāhmaṇārthe gavārthe vā dehatyāgo ‘nupaskṛtaḥ ||
dhanādyalobho ‘nupaskara iti medhātithiḥ |
strībālābhyavapattau ca balināṃ siddhikāraṇam |
strībālādivipattau ca dehatyāga ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | siddhiḥ śreyaḥ | uśanā |
sīsam ābharaṇaṃ teṣāṃ kālāyasam athāpi vā |
vadhrīṃ kaṇṭhe samābadhya jhallarīṃ kakṣato ‘pi vā ||
malāpakarṣaṇaṃ kuryuḥ pūrvāhṇe pariśuddhikam |
piṇḍībhūtā vaseyus te noced vadhyā viśeṣataḥ ||
[271]
tathā vrātyotpannāḥ śūdrasamāḥ |
tān āha manuḥ |
vrātyāt tu jāyate viprāt pāpātmā bhṛjjakaṇṭakaḥ |
āvantyavāṭadhānau ca puṣpaśekhara eva ca ||
viprāyāṃ jāyate vrātyaviprajo bhṛjjakaṇṭakaḥ |
āvantyādyās tasyaiva saṃjñā iti medhātithiḥ | anye tu bhṛjjakaṇṭakād viprāyām āvantyaḥ, tasmāt tasyāṃ vāṭadhānaḥ, tasmāc ca puṣpaśekhara ity āhuḥ | evam agre ‘pi | viśvaṃbhare etān uktvā,
etais tu jīvanārthāya kāryaṃ viṣṇvādipūjanam |
nṛtyagītādivāditraśaṅkhavīṇādi vādanam ||
jhallo mallaś ca rājanyād vrātyād vicchibir eva ca |
naṭaś ca karaṇaś caiva khaṃso dramiḍa eva ca ||
kṣatriyāyāṃ vrātyakṣatrajā ete | ayam anyaḥ karaṇo ‘nulomāt |
vaiśyāt tu jāyate vrātyāt sudhanvācārya eva ca |
kārūṣaś ca vijanmā ca maitraḥ sātvata eva ca ||
vaiśyāyām eva | ādityapurāṇe |
brāhmaṇyāṃ brāhmaṇād vrātyāj [272] jāyate mṛjjakaṇṭakaḥ |
āvantyavāṭadhānau ca puṣpaśekharajātayaḥ ||
ābhicārikakarmātra bhṛjjakaṇṭakino ‘śanam |
mantrauṣadhīśātravāṇāṃ saṃmukhīkaraṇād ṛte ||
kurvanti rājñāṃ hi tatas tenaivāvikṛtāḥ paraiḥ |
avikṛtāḥ adaṇḍyāḥ | saṃmukhīkaraṇaṃ vaśīkaraṇam |
rājñīṣu kṣatriyād vrātyāj jhallamallanaṭā jhaṣāḥ ||
āvitthibīkāḥ karaṇā dramiḍāḥ santi bhūriśaḥ |
vijñeyāḥ pararāṣṭrāṇāṃ taiś ca rājājñayā kriyāḥ ||
khaso mallas tathā vṛttaṃ mitrāmitratvam eva ca |
vaiśyastrīṣu tathā vrātyād vaiśyāt kāruṣako bhavet ||
catuṣpathe śmaśāne ca sa bhaven mitramaṇḍalam |
tatra lokavṛttāntajño rājājñayā syād ity arthaḥ | mātṛmaṇḍana iti pāṭhe devīpūjakaḥ |
sudhanvācāryas tv aparo bhaved rudrasya pūjakaḥ ||
tṛtīyaś ca vijanmākhyaḥ sūtikāveśmapūjakaḥ |
tatstrī sāṃbikā syād ity arthaḥ |
bhūtapretapiśācāṃś ca pūjayed rājaśāsanāt ||
sādhyānāṃ pūjako maitraś caityādīnāṃ caturthakaḥ |
pañcamaḥ sātvato nāma viṣṇor āyatanārcakaḥ ||
ebhyo ‘nyonyasaṃkare nīcā jāyante | tad āha manuḥ |
yathaiva śūdro brāhmaṇyāṃ bāhyaṃ jantuṃ prasūyate |
tathā brāhyāntaraṃ bāhyaś cāturvarṇye prasūyate ||
varṇānām anyonyasaṃkare bhedān āha manuḥ |
pratikūlaṃ vartamānā bāhyā bāhyāntarān punaḥ |
hīnā hīnān prasūyante varṇāḥ pañcadaśaiva tu ||
bāhyāś caṇḍālādyāḥ | pratikūlaṃ cāturvarṇyastriyo gacchantaḥ | svato bāhyāntarān nīcān janayanti | te punar uccanīcastriyo gaccnato hīnān ahīnāṃś ca suvate | varṇā jātayaḥ | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ | viprād anulomā eva | śūdrāt pratilomā eva | madhyamayor ubhayor ubhaye | kṣatrasya dvāv anulomau ekaḥ pratilomaḥ | vaiśyasyaiko ‘nulomo dvau pratilomau | atra śūdrād viprākṣatriyāvaiśyāsu kramāc caṇḍālakṣattāyogavās trayaḥ caṇḍālād viprādistrīṣu catvāraḥ | evaṃ kṣattur āyogavād iti dvādaśa | te ca svayaṃ traya iti pañcadaśa | tathā vaiśyāt kṣatriyāyāṃ māgadho viprāyāṃ vaidehakaḥ | śūdrāyāṃ karaṇo ‘nulomaḥ | taiś caturvarṇastrīṣūtpāditāḥ pratyekaṃ catvāra iti dvādaśa | svayaṃ ca te traya iti pañcadaśa | tathā kṣatriyād vaiśyāyāṃ māhiṣyaḥ | śūdrāyām ugraḥ | viprāyāṃ sūtaḥ pratilomaḥ | tebhyaś caturvarṇastrīṣu pratyekaṃ catvāra iti dvādaśa | svayaṃ ca te traya iti pañcadaśa | evaṃ viprāt trayo ‘nulomāḥ | mūdhāvasiktāmbaṣṭhapārasavāś caturvarṇastrīṣu pratyekaṃ caturo janayantīti dvādaśa | te ca traya iti pañcadaśeti sarve ṣaṣṭisaṃkhyāḥ | duṣṭāḥ catvāro varṇāś ceti catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ | jātiviveke tv anyathoktam |
ānulomyena varṇānāṃ ṣaṇ narāḥ saṃprakīrtitāḥ |
prātilomyena te ṣaṭ syur iti dvādaśa bhedataḥ ||
ete dvādaśa miśrās tu caturvarṇair vimiśritāḥ |
aṣṭau ca ye syur bhedās te ṣaṣṭir dvādaśasaṃyutāḥ ||
hatās te ṣaṣṭisaṃkhyātaiḥ ṣaṭtriśacchatasaṃkhyayā |
bhedāḥ saṃkarajātīnāṃ bahavaḥ syus tataḥ pare || iti |
ete sūdrasamāḥ,
śūdrāṇāṃ tu sadharmāṇaḥ sarve ‘padhvaṃsajāḥ smṛtāḥ |
iti manūkteḥ | apadhvaṃsajā vyabhicārajātāḥ |
[274]
saṃkarajasvarūpaṃ vṛttayaś ca |
etatsvarūpaṃ vṛttayaś coktāḥ ādipurāṇe ‘pi |
mūrdhāvasikto viprād yaḥ kṣatriyāyāṃ prasūyate |
sa karoti manuṣyāṇāṃ cikitsāṃ rogiṇām api ||
apiśabdāt kṣātraṃ karma ca |
brāhmaṇāc chūdrakanyāyāṃ bhavet pārasavas tu saḥ |
niṣādo matyaghātitvaṃ cakre rājājñayā sadā ||
brāhmaṇenograkanyāyām ambaṣṭho nāma jāyate |
sa karoti manuṣyāṇāṃ cikitsāṃ rogiṇām api ||
viprād vaiśyājaḥ kṣatrāc chūdrājaś ceti dvāv ambaṣṭhau |
brāhmaṇena tathāmbaṣṭhyām ābhīro jāyate sutaḥ |
go’jāvirakṣāṃ kurute sa ca rājagṛheṣv api ||
āyogavyāṃ brāhmaṇāt tu jāyate dhigvaṇo naraḥ |
vaiśyāyāṃ śūdrajā āyogavī |
sa carmakāro vijñeyo garhitaś carmajīvanaḥ ||
ete mātṛsadharmāṇo na pituḥ putrakāś ca ṣaṭ |
mūrdhāvasiktapārasavāmbaṣṭhābhīracarmakārāḥ prāg uktā avaroṭaś ceti ṣaṭ |
mūrdhāvasikto ‘mbaṣṭhaś ca niṣādaś ca tathā trayaḥ ||
viprakṣatriyaviṭśūdranārīṇāṃ gamanād iha |
ekaikaśas tu caturo janayanti sutān api ||
ete dvādaśa vidyante mūlaprakṛtibhir yutāḥ |
bhavanti pañcadaśa vai vipraḥ ṣoḍaśako bhavet ||
mūrdhāvasiktād viprāyāṃ pratilomaḥ | kṣatrādiṣv anulomā iti [275] catvāraḥ | evam ambaṣṭān niṣādāc ceti dvādaśa | svayaṃ caite traya iti pañcadaśa | vrātyo vipraḥ avaroṭo vā ṣoḍaśaḥ |
dasyusaṃjñā bhavanty ete mātṛvat karmakāriṇaḥ |
rājño brāhmaṇakanyāyāṃ sūto varṇas tu jāyate ||
sa rājñām aśvasārathyaṃ cakre dakṣaś ca nityaśaḥ |
sūtaḥ prajāpatis tv anyo māyārūpī janārdanaḥ ||
vaktā vedādiśāstrāṇāṃ purāṇānāṃ ca sa smṛtaḥ |
anya iti nāmnaiva sūto na tu jātyety arthaḥ | ugrādisaṃjñāvat | sūtaputra ugraśravāḥ sautiḥ paurāṇiko ‘pi | tathā |
kṣatriyād vaiśyakanyāyāṃ māhiṣyo nāma jāyate |
nakṣatragānanṛtyajño dhanānāṃ parirakṣitā ||
rājñas tu śūdrakanyāyām ugrākhyo nāma jāyate |
durgāntaḥpurakośānāṃ rakṣāṃ vipreṣu vācanam ||
ugro duṣṭaś ca dasyūnāṃ kaścid anyo ‘pi vidyate |
sūtamāhiṣyakogrās tu trayo varṇacatuṣṭayāt ||
ekaikaśas tu caturo janayanti sutān api |
ātmanā saha rājñā tu tatra ṣoḍaśa santi te ||
devalaḥ |
devabrāhmaṇbālastrīdīnāndhakṛpaṇais tu yat |
upārjitaṃ dhanaṃ tasya parityāgāt tu dasyavaḥ ||
brāhmaṇyām atha vaiśyena vaideho nāma jāyate |
ete dvādaśa vidyante mūlaprakṛtibhir yutāḥ ||
bhavanti pañcadaśa vai vipraḥ ṣoḍaśako bhavet |
rakṣitā rājadurgāṇāṃ tadvṛttiparasaṃyutaḥ ||
kṣatriyāyāṃ tu vaiśyena mucuko nāmato bhavet |
jale sthale ca yo mārgas tatra rakṣāṃ karoti saḥ ||
rājadūto vādyajīvo bhavet kīrtiprakāśakaḥ |
ayam eva bandī |
vaiśyena śūdryāṃ karaṇo varṇaḥ syāt prakaroti saḥ ||
durgāntaḥpuragañjānāṃ rakṣāṃ brāhmaṇasaṃgraham |
gañjā tu madirāgṛham ity amaraḥ | saṃgraho rakṣaṇam |
vaidehamāgadhāv etau karaṇaś ceti te trayaḥ ||
varṇebhyaḥ pūrvavat putrān janayanti vimohitaḥ |
te ca ṣoḍaśa jāyante mūlaprakṛtibhiḥ saha ||
duṣṭāni vaiśyakarmāṇi te ‘pi kurvanti dasyavaḥ |
brāhmaṇyām atha śūdreṇa caṇḍālaḥ syāj jugupsitaḥ ||
sa cakre rājamārgādiśodhanaṃ ca kukarma ca |
rājastriyāṃ ca śūdreṇa kṣattā maindaś ca jāyate ||
śvāvidhyam atha godhānāṃ sa cakre vadhabandhanam |
āyogavas tu śūdreṇa vaiśyāyāṃ tu bhavec ca saḥ ||
takṣā sthapatir ity ukto rājñāṃ veśmakaraḥ sadā |
dārutakṣakas takṣā | pāṣāṇabhedakaḥ sthapatir iti dvāv apy etad bhedau |
āyogavas tathā kṣattā caṇḍālaś cāpi te trayaḥ ||
caturvarṇāsu tanayān janayanty atha pūrvavat |
śūdreṇa sahitās te tu santi ṣoḍaśa saṃkhyayā ||
duṣṭāni yāni karmāṇi te ‘pi kurvanti dasyavaḥ |
ete viprādayo jñeyās catuḥṣaṣṭiṣu yoniṣu ||
utpādayanti sadṛśān saṃkīrṇān anyayoniṣu |
atra svabhinnāsu triṣaṣṭibhir janitās tāsu vā tair janitā ekaikasya ṣaḍviṃśatyadhikaṃ śataṃ bhedāḥ | te ca catuḥṣaṣṭibhir guṇitāḥ aṣṭasahasrāṇi catuḥṣaṣṭiś ca bhavanti | teṣāṃ varṇasaṃkīrṇasaṃkareṇānyonyasaṃkarāc cānantyaṃ bhavati | vistarāpatter nocyate | manuḥ |
śanakais tu kriyālopād imāḥ kṣatriyajātayaḥ |
vṛṣalatvaṃ gatā loke brāhmaṇātikrameṇa ca ||
vyabhicāreṇa varṇānām avedyāvedanena ca |
saṃgena dharmatyāgena jāyate varṇasaṃkaraḥ ||
saṃgo mlecchādinīcasaṃgatiḥ | eteṣu kāṃścid āha manuḥ |
prasādhanopacārajñam adāsaṃ dāsajīvinam |
sairandhraṃ vāgurāvṛttiṃ sūte dasyum ayogavī ||
[276] vaiśyāyāṃ śūdrajāyogavī | dasyor varṇasaṃkarajān sūte | te ca dasyavo manūktāḥ |
pauṇḍrakāś coṇḍradramiḍāḥ kāmbojā yavanāḥ śakāḥ |
pāradāḥ pahlavāś cīnāḥ kirātā daradās tathā ||
mukhabāhūrujātānāṃ yā loke jātayo bahiḥ |
mlecchavācoryavāco vā sarve te dasyavaḥ smṛtāḥ ||
ete mlecchabhedāḥ | prasādhanaṃ maṇḍanam | upacāro ‘ṅgamardanādi | vasiṣṭhas tu vṛttyantaram āha |
sairandhāṇāṃ surāmadyasīdhukaraṇaṃ tadvikrayaḥ puṣpaphalaśākavikrayaś ca | iti |
manuḥ |
maitreyakaṃ tu vaideho mādhūkaṃ saṃprasūyate |
nṝn praśaṃsaty ajasraṃ yo ghaṇṭāvādo ‘ruṇodaye ||
bhavanty āyogavīṣv ete iti manūkter vaiśyāyāṃ śūdrajāyogavyāṃ viprāyāṃ vaiśyajād vaidehān maitreyaṃ sūte | sa eva mādhūkaḥ |
niṣādo mārgaraṃ sūte dāśaṃ naukarmajīvinam |
kaivartam iti yaṃ prāhur āryāvartanivāsinaḥ ||
kṣatrāyāṃ śūdrajān niṣādāt prāguktāyogavyāṃ daśa ity arthaḥ |
brāhmaṇād ity anuvṛttau manuḥ |
ābhīro ‘mbaṣṭhakanyāyām āyogavyāṃ tu dhigvaṇaḥ |
vaiśyāyāṃ śūdrajāyogavī | tasyām viprajo dhigvaṇaḥ | vaiśyāyāṃ viprajāmbaṣṭhī | tasyām viprād ābhīraḥ | ayaṃ gopālānulomaḥ prasaṃgād uktaḥ | jātiviveke |
vartanaṃ gomahiṣyādi poṣayet tṛṇavāribhiḥ |
payo dadhi ghṛtaṃ takraṃ vikretavyaṃ dhanārthibhiḥ ||
[279]
atha bhojyānnāḥ |
asya bhojyānnatvam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
śūdreṣu dāsagopālakulamitrārdhasīriṇaḥ |
bhojyānnā nāpitaś caiva yaś cātmānaṃ nivedayet ||
śūdrānnabhakṣaṇe manuḥ |
rājānnaṃ teja ādatte śūdrānnam brahmavarcasam |
āyuḥ suvarṇakārānnaṃ yaśaś carmāvakartinaḥ ||
bhuktvāto ‘nyatamasyānnam amatyā kṣapaṇaṃ tryaham |
matyā tu bhakṣaṇae kṛcchraṃ retoviṇmutram eva ca ||
yat tu śaṅkhaḥ |
śūdrānnaṃ brāhmaṇo bhuktvā tathā raṅgāvatāriṇaḥ |
cikitsakasya krūrasya tathā strīmṛgajīvinaḥ ||
caṇḍālānnaṃ bhūmipānnam ajajīviśvajīvinām |
śauṇḍikānnaṃ sūtikānnaṃ bhuktvā māsaṃ vrataṃ caret ||
vrataṃ gomūtrayāvakam | idam abhyāse | āmānne tu tṛtīyāṃśaḥ | caṇḍālānnaṃ bhuktvā trirātram upavaset siddhānnaṃ bhuktvā parākam iti mādhavīye caṇḍālānne viṣṇunā tathokteḥ | caṇḍālānnam āmam | āpadi tu parāśaraḥ |
āpatkāle tu vipreṇa bhuktaṃ śūdragṛhe yadi |
manastāpena śudhyeta drupadānāṃ śataṃ japet ||
idam āmānnaparam,
nādyāc chūdrasya pakvānnaṃ vidvān aśrāddhino dvijaḥ |
ādadītāmam evāsmād vṛttau caikarātrikam ||
iti manūkteḥ | yat tu yājñavalkyaḥ,
śūdreṣu dāsagopālakulamitrārdhasīriṇaḥ |
bhojyānnā nāpitaś caiva yaś cātmānaṃ nivedayet ||
iti, yaś ca devalaḥ,
svadāso nāpito gopaḥ kumbhakāraḥ kṛṣīvalaḥ |
brāhmaṇair api bhojyānnāḥ pañcaite śūdrayonayaḥ ||
iti, atrānnaśabdo ‘danīyamātraparo na tv odanaparaḥ | yat tu bhaviṣye,
upakṣepaṇadharmeṇa śūdrānnaṃ yaḥ paced dvijaḥ |
abhojyaṃ tad bhaved annaṃ sa ca vipraḥ purohitaḥ ||
upakṣepaṇaṃ śūdreṇa svānnasya pākārthaṃ vipragṛhe samarpaṇam iti kalpataruḥ | idaṃ śuśrūṣakabhinnaparam,
triṣu varṇeṣu kartavyaṃ pakvabhojanam eva ca |
śuśrūṣām abhipannānāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ ca varānane ||
iti vārāhokteḥ | tena devebhyo naivedyam api pakveneti gaṅgāvākyāvalī | sarvaṃ kalītaraparam iti śuddhitattve uktam | saṃsṛṣṭaduṣṭabhojya ‘nne viśeṣam āha aparārke śūlapāṇau ca sumantuḥ |
gorasaṃ caiva saktūṃś ca tailaṃ piṇyākam eva ca |
apūpān bhakṣayec chūdrād yac cānyat payasā kṛtam ||
payanānyāsaṃsṛṣṭadugdhena kṛtaṃ pākaṃ vinā | tena pāyase doṣa eva, payaseti tṛtīyayā nairapekṣyāvagateḥ, śūdrajale doṣokteś ca | yac ceti cakārād apūpeṣu payaḥkṛteṣv eva | tena jalakṛte polikādau doṣa evety arthaḥ | atredaṃ tattvam | dvividho dvijaḥ | śūdrānnaratas tannivṛttaś ca | ādyasya pūrvoktaṃ śūdrānnaṃ bhojyam | ata eva aparārke śūlapāṇau ca hārītaḥ |
kandupakvaṃ snehapakvaṃ pāyasaṃ dadhi saktavaḥ |
etāni śūdrānnabhujo bhojyāni manur abravīt ||
dvijair etāni bhojyāni śūdragehakṛtāny apīti śuddhitattve kaurmottarārdham | pāyasaṃ pākaṃ vinā [280] kṣīreṇa kṛtam,
āmaṃ śūdrasya pakvānnaṃ pakvam ucchiṣṭam ucyate |
iti śātātapokteḥ, “yac cānyat payasā kṛtam” iti pūrvam ukteḥ | idaṃ śūdragehe ‘pi bhojyam | tad uktaṃ śūlapāṇau brāhme |
rājñāṃ parvaṇi vaiśyānām aśnīyān maṅgale gavām |
vāsobhūratnahemānnaṃ sacchūdrasya gṛhe tathā || iti |
idam api kalau varjyam,
śūdreṣu sāsagopālakulamitrārdhasīriṇām |
bhojānnatā gṛhasthasya tīrthasevātidūrataḥ ||
brāhmaṇādiṣu śūdrasya pakvatādikriyāpi vā |
iti ca mādhavīye pṛthvīcandrodaye ca ādityapurāṇat | śūdrānnanivṛttasya tu śūlapāṇāv aṅgirāḥ |
pātrāntaragataṃ grāhyaṃ śūdrāt svagṛham āgatam |
śūdraveśmani vipreṇa kṣīraṃ vā yadi vā dadhi ||
nivṛttena na bhoktavyaṃ śūdrānnaṃ tad api smṛtam |
viṣṇupurāṇe |
saṃprokṣayitvā gṛhṇīyāc chūdrānnaṃ gṛham āgatam |
yat tu yājñavalkyaḥ,
vikarmasthān dvijān śūdrān savāsā jalam āviśet |
iti śūdrasparśe snānam āha, tad dāsādibhinnaśūdraparaṃ vā, anyathā
mūlyakarmakarāḥ śūdradāsīdāsās tathaiva ca |
snāne śarīrasaṃskāre gṛhakarmaṇy adūṣitāḥ ||
sadyaḥspṛśyo garbhadāso bhaktadāsas tryahāc chuciḥ |
ityādivirodhāt | manuḥ |
abhojyānāṃ tu bhuktvānnaṃ strīśūdrocchiṣṭam eva ca |
jagdhvā māṃsas abhakṣyaṃ ca saptarātraṃ yavān pibet ||
jñāne dviguṇam |
[282]
viśvaṃbharaśāstre |
māhiṣyāyāṃ dvijāj jāta ābhīraḥ kṣīravikrayī |
udavāhaḥ suto viprād vaidehyāṃ vyabhicārataḥ ||
sa chatraṃ dhārayet kuryān narāṇāṃ vikrayaṃ tathā |
kṣatrād vaiśyāyāṃ māhiṣyā | vaiśyād viprāyāṃ vaidehī |
viprān niṣādyāṃ yo jātaḥ pauṣṭikaḥ skandhavāhakaḥ ||
śūdryāṃ viprān niṣādī |
ambaṣṭhāyāṃ dvijāj jātaḥ putraḥ kāṃsyasya karmakṛt |
[283]        kāṃsyapātrāṇi citrāṇi racayej jīvanāya saḥ ||
śūdradharmeṇa sarvatra sthitir asya vidhīyate |
ugrāyāṃ yo dvijāj jātaḥ kumbhakāraḥ sa ucyate ||
sa śūdrād dhīyate dharme ghaṭayen mṛnmayān ghaṭān |
jātiviveke |
vrātyaḥ kṣatriyataḥ śūdryāṃ jātaḥ śūdravidhānakṛt |
sa kuryād rājaputrāṃś ca śastrāstrakuśalān dhanam ||
tebhyo labdhvātmavṛttyarthaṃ svadharmam anupālayet |
rājaguruḥ |
tasmān mallaḥ suto jātaḥ kṣatriṇyāṃ vyabhicārataḥ |
rājñāṃ kautukam āpādya niyuddhenārjayed dhanam ||
vaiśyāc chūdryām athāmbaṣṭhyāṃ jāto vaitālikābhidhaḥ ||
jīvanaṃ kāmaśāstreṇa stutyā vā bandikarmaṇā |
kṣatrāt pārasavīputraḥ kiṃnāṭas tāmrakuṭṭakaḥ ||
vaṇigjanāt tu viprāyāṃ jāto ‘ntaḥpurapālakaḥ |
brāhmaṇyāṃ jāyate vaiśyād yo ‘sau vaidehakābhidhaḥ ||
śuddhāntarakṣaṇaṃ rājñāṃ kuryād anupamaṃ hi saḥ |
sāmānyavanitāḥ poṣyās tāsām ādyā ca jīvikā ||
paṇyāṅganānāṃ rājñāṃ ca kuryāt saṅgaṃ tadicchayā |
rūpājīvāsu tāsv eva viśiṣyāsaṃgato viṭaḥ ||
sa eva tāsāṃ prāṇeśo nānyaḥ kānto ‘pi tatpatiḥ |
catuḥṣaṣṭikalāḥ kāmaśāstraṃ tadupajīvanam ||
catuḥṣaṣṭikalā bharatenoktāḥ |
vedāṅgāgamaśilpaśāstrakavitā vaktṛtvatauryatrika-
dyūtāstrādbhutavādasūparacanāś citraṃ paṇo vaidyakam |
nānāratnapurāṇalekhapuruṣastrīdātubhāṣālipi-
prajñānāṭakakelidantituragaprajñā ca sāmudrikam ||
śayyābhojanayuddhamardanavibhūṣābandhasarveṅgita-
jñānaṃ rajjutarīkalārathakṛtiḥ saukhye ‘ṣṭabhogeṣu dhīḥ |
trāṇe vāriviṣāgnivāriṣu rathārevāṅgaśīrṣeṣu
yad durgārāmakṛtiḥ paśudviyanametkāraḥ (?) śivārcādayaḥ ||
sugandhaṃ vanitā vastraṃ gītaṃ tāmbūlabhojanam |
śayyā vibhūṣā surataṃ bhogāṣṭakam udāhṛtam ||
śūdrād vaiśyāyāṃ yo jāto vaṇigjana iti smṛtaḥ |
pārasavyāṃ ca māhiṣyān mālākāraḥ sa ucyate ||
kusumāni saśākāni vardhayed dhanavṛddhaye |
sa hīnaḥ śūdradharmebhyaḥ samūhe padmake prabhuḥ ||
sūtāj jātas tu vaidehyāṃ sūtakāraḥ sa jāyate |
āyogavyāṃ tu karaṇāt sairandhro gandhakarmakṛt ||
puṣpacandanapaṇyādiśṛṅgārais tasya jīvanam |
pādayoḥ kṣālanaṃ caiva dhambhillānāṃ prasādhanam ||
pratyaṅgamardanaṃ caiva candanenānulepanam |
mṛganābheś candrayogaṃ śṛṅgāraracanāṃ navām ||
ājīvanaṃ tu tasyoktaṃ tatstrī sairandhrikā smṛtā |
viśambharaśāstre |
prasādhanopacaturā sairandhrīti prakīrtitā |
śaivaḥ pāśupataḥ kaścid ārūḍhaḥ patito yadi ||
jaṅgama iti prasiddhaḥ |
tasmāj jātaḥ śūdrapatnyāṃ putro bhasmāṅkuraḥ smṛtaḥ |
śivārcanaṃ tu tat kāryaṃ śivadāyena jīvanam ||
sa jaṭābhasyadhārī ca śivaliṅgaṃ prapūjayet |
tāmbūlam akṣatā dravyaṃ gāvaḥ kṣetrāṇi kākiṇī ||
śivāya prāṇibhir dattam anyat kiṃ api bhaktitaḥ |
caṇḍīśaṃ yat kṣitau khyātaṃ tena tasyeha jīvikā ||
kuśīlavaḥ suto ‘mbaṣṭād vaidehyāṃ nartanādikṛt |
jātiviveke |
māhiṣyā māgadhā caiva śuddhamārgakasaṃjñakam |
janayet tanayaṃ so ‘pi śūdradharmavinākṛtaḥ ||
gītaṃ caturvidhaṃ vādyam abhyasej jīvanāya saḥ |
saṃgītaśāstre |
tatrādau trividhaṃ gītaṃ vādyaṃ cāpi caturvidham |
caturdaśavidhaṃ nṛtyaṃ gītapūrvaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ ||
caturvidhaṃ vādyam āha amarasiṃhaḥ |
tataṃ vīṇādikaṃ vādyam ānaddhaṃ murajādikam |
vaṃśādikaṃ tu suṣiraṃ kāṃsyatālādikaṃ ghanam ||
ayaṃ mṛdaṅgajīvī |
nirmaṇḍalaka ābhīrāj jāto ‘sau bāṇapicchakṛt |
śarāṇāṃ śarapatraiś ca racanā tasya jīvikā ||
ugrāyāṃ māgadhāj jātaḥ kuntalaśmaśrukarmakṛt |
sa nāpita iti proktaḥ kṣaurakarmavidhānakṛt ||
kṣatrāc chūdrāja ugraḥ | vaiśyāt kṣatrājo māgadhaḥ |
śmaśrukartanakṛc caiva nakhakartanakovidaḥ |
vṛttyānayā grāmamadhye tiṣthed varṇeṣu sevakaḥ ||
narāṇāṃ nāpito [285] dhūrtaḥ śūdrebhyo ‘bhyadhikaḥ smṛtaḥ |
vṛddhamanuḥ |
śūdrakanyāsamutpanno brāḥmaṇena tu saṃskṛtaḥ |
aparo nāpitaḥ proktaḥ śūdradharmādhiko ‘pi saḥ ||
yājñavalkyena tu śūdra eva nāpita uktaḥ |
śūdreṣu dāsagopālakulamitrārdhasīriṇaḥ |
bhojyānnā nāpitaś caiveti |
śaṅkhena tu pratilomajo ‘yam uktaḥ | yat tu sūtasaṃhitāyām ūrdhvanāpito ‘dhonāpitaś ceti dvaividhyam uktaṃ tad vyavasthitaṃ jñeyam |
[287]
atha kāyasthotpattiḥ |
pādme sṛṣṭikhaṇḍe |
sṛṣṭyādau sadasatkarmajñaptaye prāṇināṃ vidhiḥ |
kṣaṇaṃ dhyāne sthitas tasya sarvakāyād vinirgataḥ ||
divyarūpaḥ pumān haste maṣīpātraṃ ca lekhanīm |
citragupta iti khyāto dharmarājasamīpataḥ ||
prāṇināṃ sadasatkarmalekhyāya sa niyojitaḥ |
brahmaṇātīndriyajñānī devāgryo yajñabhuk sadā ||
bhojanāc ca sadā tasmād āhutir dīyate dvijaiḥ |
brahmakāyodbhavo yasmāt kāyastho jātir ucyate ||
nānāgotrāś ca tadvaṃśyāḥ kāyasthā bhuvi santi vai |
skānde reṇukāmāhātmye |
evaṃ hatvārjunaṃ rāmaḥ saṃdhāya niśitān śarān |
anvadhāvat sa tān hantuṃ sarvān evāturān nṛpān ||
kecid gahanam āśritya kecit pātālam āviśan |
sagarbhā candrasenasya bhāryā dālbhyāśramaṃ yayau ||
tato rāmaḥ samāyāto dālbhyāśramam anuttamam |
pūjito muninā rāmo hy arghyapādyāsanādibhiḥ ||
dadau madhyāhnasamaye tasmai bhojanam ādarāt |
rāmas tu yācayāmāsa hṛdisthaṃ svamanoratham ||
yācayāmāsa rāmāc ca kāmaṃ dālbhyo mahāmuniḥ |
tato dvau paramaprītau bhojanaṃ cakratur mudā ||
bhojanānantaraṃ dālbhyaḥ papraccha bhārgavaṃ prati |
yat tvayā prārthitaṃ deva tattvaṃ śaṃsitum arhasi ||
rāma uvāca |
tavāśrame mahābhāga sagarbhā strī samāgatā |
candrasenasya rājarṣeḥ kṣatriyasya mahātmanaḥ ||
tan me tvaṃ prārthitaṃ dehi hiṃseyaṃ tāṃ mahāmune |
tato dālbhyaḥ pratyuvāca dadāmi tava vāñchitam ||
yan mayā prārthitaṃ deva tan me dātuṃ tvam arhasi |
tataḥ striyaṃ samāhuya candrasenasya vai muniḥ ||
rāmāya pradadau bhītāṃ rāmaḥ prītamanā abhūt |
rāma uvāca |
yat tvayā prārthitaṃ vipra bhojanāvasare purā |
tan me śaṃsa mahābhāga dadāmi tava vāñchitam ||
dālbhya uvāca |
striyo garbham amuṃ [288] bālaṃ tan me tvaṃ dātum arhasi |
tato rāmo ‘bravīd dālbhyaṃ yadartham aham āgataḥ ||
kṣatriyāntakaraś cāhaṃ taṃ tvaṃ yācitavān asi |
prārthitaś ca tvayā vipra kāyastho garbha uttamaḥ ||
tasmāt kāyastha ity ākhyā bhaviṣyati śiśoḥ śubhā |
kāyastha eṣa utpannaḥ kṣatriṇyāṃ kṣatriyāt tataḥ ||
rāmājñayā sa dālbhyena kṣātradharmād bahiṣkṛtaḥ |
dattaḥ kāyasthadharmo ‘smai citraguptasya yaḥ smrtaḥ ||
tadgotrajāś ca kāyasthā dālbhyagotrās tato ‘bhavan |
dālbhyopadeśakās te vai dharmiṣṭhāḥ satyavādinaḥ ||
sadācāraparā nityaṃ ratā hariharārcane |
devaviprapitṝṇāṃ vai atithīnāṃ ca pūjakāḥ ||
māhiṣyavanitā sūnuṃ vaidehādyaṃ prasūyate |
sa kāyastha iti proktas tasya karma vidhīyate ||
kṣatrād vaiśyāyāṃ māhiṣyā | viprāyāṃ vaiśyajo vaidehaḥ |
lipīnāṃ deśajātānāṃ lekhanaṃ sa samācaret |
gaṇakatvaṃ vicitraṃ ca bījapāṭhī prabhedataḥ ||
adhamaḥ śūdrajātibhyaḥ pañcasaṃskāravān asau |
cāturvarṇyasya sevā hi lipilekhanasādhanam ||
vyavasāyaḥ śilpakarma tajjīvanam udāhṛtam |
śikhāṃ yajñopavītaṃ ca vastram āraktam ambhasā ||
sparśanaṃ devatānāṃ ca kāyasthād yo vivarjayet |
iti kāyasthaḥ |
[289]
atha saṃkīrṇaprakaraṇam |
mālākārāc ca lāvadyaḥ karaṇyāṃ maṇirandhrakṛt |
maṇihāraḥ mañjur iti cocyate |
kunālyāṃ[95] śālmalo mañjor nāgavalyās tu vikrayī ||
tāmbūlakrītasaṃbhūtaṃ dravyaṃ tasyeha jīvanam |
mālākārāt kṣatriyāyāṃ śilīndhro mardanakriyaḥ ||
hīnaḥ sa śūdradharmebhyo jīvikāsyāṅgamardanam |
śilīndhrāt kṣatriyāyāṃ ca kolhāṭi nāma jāyate ||
[290] bahurūpīti praiddhaḥ |
vaiśyāt karaṇyāṃ yo jāto gocārī vatsapālakaḥ |
nāmnā chagalakaṃ putraṃ dhaṭadhānād ajījanat ||
mañjubhāryāsuto loke[96] ajapāla iti smṛtaḥ
tathā |
karmacaṇḍālabhāryāyāṃ yo jātaḥ puṣpaśekharāt |
sa vai maṇḍalako nāma śunāṃ maṇḍalapoṣakaḥ ||
yugulaṃ śunakānāṃ sa dhartuṃ yojyo mahībhṛtā |
vidhavāyāṃ yater jātaḥ karmacaṇḍāla ucyate ||
vāpīkūpādimṛtkarmakartā cāspṛśya eva saḥ |
jātiviveke |
ambaṣṭhāc caiva vaidehyāṃ jātaḥ putraḥ kuśīlavaḥ |
saṃgītajño nṛtyakartā deśād deśāntaraṃ vrajet ||
āyogavān māgadhāyāṃ bhrakuṃsa it jāyate |
sa varṇabāhyo dharmeṣu samyak saṃgītakovidaḥ ||
kāntānāṃ nṛtyaśālāyāṃ nṛtyaṃ lāsyaṃ ca śikṣayet |
raṅgāvatāraḥ bharatenokāḥ |
kāyiko vācikaś caiva āhāryaḥ sātvikas tathā |
catvāro ‘bhinayāḥ proktāḥ nāṭyaśāstraviśradaiḥ ||
saṃgītaśāstre |
gītaṃ vādyaṃ ca nṛtyaṃ ca trayaṃ saṃgītam ucyate |
saṃgītaśabdo nṛtye tu kevalenaiva gīyate ||
sukumāraṃ bhaval lāsyam uddhataṃ tāṇḍavaṃ viduḥ |
tāṇḍavaṃ padaghātāddyair lāsyaṃ rasasamanvitam ||
dṛṣṭihastakabhāṣādair bhrāmarīcīrikādibhiḥ |
kucabāhūrudṛkpātais taruṇīnāṃ tad adbhutam ||
kāyasthavanitā yā tu mālākāreṇa saṃgatā |
tasyāṃ yas tena putraḥ syāt sa śālakya iti smṛtaḥ ||
kāntāreṣu racayan gajadantakalāpitā |
sa dīnaḥ śūdrajātibhyo maṇīn viracet sadā ||
sphaṭikān[97] badarādīṃś ca kuryāt taddravyajīvikām |
vaidehāc chūdrakanyāyāṃ jātas tv āṇdhasikābhidhaḥ ||
kuryād annāni bhakṣyāṇi vikryārthaṃ sasaṃdhitam |
ato vikrayato labdhaṃ tad dhanaṃ tasya jīvanam ||
kāndū iti prasiddhaḥ |
karaṇīvaiśyasaṃyogāj jāto vatsakasaṃjñakaḥ |
sa hīnaḥ śūdradharmebhyaḥ śāḍvale gāś ca cārayet ||
vaiśyastrīśūdrasaṃyogād āyogavakasaṃjñakaḥ |
sa śūdrād dhīyate dharmaiḥ pāṣāṇeṣṭakakarmakṛt ||
sa kuryāt kuṭṭimāṃ bhūmiṃ cūrṇenāsya ca jīvanam |
niṣādakanyakā śūdrasaṃgād yaṃ janayet sutam ||
sa krodhakaḥ kukkuṭa iti prokto dvisaṃjñakaḥ |
ṭaṅkaśālāsu sarvatra nāṇakānāṃ vidhāyakaḥ ||
yo māgadhīkṣatriyayor jāta ulmukasaṃjñakaḥ |
sa lohakarmaṇā jīved uttamaś cāntyajātitaḥ ||
lohakāra iti khyāto lauhaṃ karmāsya jīvanam |
teṣāṃ tu guṇasaṃyogaṃ jñātvā śastravidhāyakaḥ ||
lakṣaṇakāṇḍe |
śastrāṅgulaṃ ṣaṭsametaṃ saptahṛcchedakābhidhaḥ |
śastrasvāmīśayor vairaṃ mṛtykāry anyathā śubham ||
iti lohakārasmṛtiḥ |
tailabhramaṇayantrāṇāṃ yāvac chabdaḥ pravartate |
tāvat karma na kurvīrta śūdrāntyajasamīpataḥ ||
kukkuṭasyeha bhavati kumbhakāreṇa saṃgatā |
tasyāḥ sūnus tu vikhyātaḥ kuruvinda iti sphuṭam ||
kauśeyāni sa vastrāṇi racayed ātmavṛttaye |
tulyo ‘sāv antyajātīnāṃ svadharmaṃ sa tu pālayet ||
sālīti prasiddhaḥ |
saṃgatā venavanitā kaivartena yadā rahaḥ |
tasyāḥ śāmbarikābhikhyaḥ putro ‘sau lokasaṃmataḥ ||
sa hīnas tv antyajātibhyaḥ śucivāsovidhāyakaḥ |
kuvindaḥ |
ābhīrīkukkuṭābhyāṃ yo jātaḥ sokhirasaṃjñakaḥ ||
sa kuryāt tu sarīṇāṃ hi vāsāṃsi svātmavṛttaye |
tadvaiparītyāj jāto yo nīlīkartā sa kathyate ||
[291]        mārganāpitayor jāto yo ‘sau sākhilyasaṃjñakaḥ |
hīnaḥ sa guhyakeśānāṃ kuryād vapanam añcasā ||
jalūkātumbiśṛṅgāṇi raktasrāve prayojayet |
jāto maitreyaśuklena jāndhikāyāṃ svayaṃ sutaḥ ||
asau bandhulsaṃjño ‘tre adhamaḥ sarvajātiṣu |
suvarṇakāravipaṇer dhūlyāṃ hemādi paśyati ||
niṣādanārīsaṃyogāt pauṣṭikaṃ janayet sutam |
sa ca pāṃsulasaṃjño hi śaṇasūtravidhāyakaḥ ||
kartā ca gauṇīpaṭṭānāṃ jīvikā tasya tad dhanam |
pulkasastrī pāṃsulakāt sūte yā vāsikābhidham ||
kuryāt sa turagādīnāṃ śaṣpeṇaiva ca vartanam |
ghāsī |
medasya vanitākāyāt saṃgatāndhreṇa ced rahaḥ ||
sā sūte yavanaṃ putraṃ turuṣkaḥ sa prakīrtitaḥ |
prasiddho mlecchadeśe yo govadhenāsya vartanam ||
bandinīśūdrasaṃyogāj jātaḥ spandālikābhidhaḥ |
caturvarṇavihīno ‘sau mañjiṣṭhāraṅgakārakaḥ ||
tena raṅgeṇa vāsāṃsi citrāṇi racayet sadā |
hastalekhyaṃ vicitraṃ ca dvidhā tac citrasādhanam ||
sa eva śucikaḥ khyātaḥ kartarīsūcikārjakaḥ |
māgadhyāṃ kṣatriyāj jāta ulmuko lohakarmakṛt ||
ugrāt pārasavīputro maukālis tailiko bhuvi |
rajako varṇato hīnaḥ prathamaś cāntyajeṣu saḥ ||
vastranirṇejanaṃ kuryād ātmavṛttyartham eva ca |
ugrajātaṃ tu vaidehyāṃ mañjūṣaṃ rajakaṃ viduḥ ||
śūdrāyāṃ kṣatrād ugraḥ | viprāyāṃ vaiśyād vaidehī |
vahed asau tailayantram uttamaś cāntyajātitaḥ |
tailahiṃsāyantraravākarṣaṇāt pāpasaṃbhavaḥ ||
ato maukaliko nityaṃ nirvāsyo nagarād bahiḥ |
rathakārasya duhitā sāyogavasamāgatā ||
sūte yaṃ tanayaṃ so ‘pi sūtradhāra itīritaḥ |
jāyājīvaś ca śailūṣo nāṭyaśāstraviśāradaḥ ||
venyām āvarttakāj jātaḥ sa cāraḥ sūtravāyakaḥ |
chāgalī bhṛjjakaṇṭāt tu aurabhraṃ meṣapālakam ||
kuryād ūrṇāpaṭāṃś citrān meṣāṇāṃ paripālanam |
āvarttakasya bhāryāyāṃ kṣemakād uṣṭrapālakam ||
raumikaṃ deśikā mallād yuktaṃ lavaṇakarmaṇi |
āyogavyāṃ dvijāj jāto dhigvaṇaḥ kavacādikṛt ||
sa carmaṇāśvapalyāṇaṃ yathāśobhaṃ prakalpayet |
mocisaṃjñaḥ |
āyogavī ca kaivarttaṃ jajñe parasavāt sutam ||
sa hīnaḥ sarvajātibhyo jālaṃ svīkṛtya sarvadā |
matsyān jalacarān anyān ghātayed ātmavṛttaye ||
nāvyaṃ karma praharaṇaṃ nadyāṃ varṣāsu vāhayet |
nadīm uttārayel lokāṃs tebhyaś cec cheddhanaṃ tathā ||
dhigvaṇyāṃ tu niṣādād yaś carmakāraḥ sa tu smṛtaḥ |
gocarmaṇā mahiṣyāś ca carmaṇā tasya jīvanam ||
ābhīrastrī labhed venāt putraṃ śauṇḍikasaṃjñakam |
surāṃ sa kṛtvā vikrīya kuryāt tena ca vartanam ||
karmacaṇḍālakāt putraṃ vaidehī pāṇḍuśopakam |
lebhe baruḍajātitvaṃ sadā vaṃśavidāraṇam ||
jāto niṣādād vaidehyāṃ gāruḍī ḍiṇdibhottamaḥ |
ityādi |
manuḥ |
caṇḍālāt pāṇḍuśopākas tvaksāravyavahāravān |
āhiṇḍako niṣādena vaidehyām eva jāyate ||
viprāyāṃ vaiśyajā vaidehī | tvaksāro veṇuḥ | tacchedakriyādijīvī | ayaṃ baruḍaḥ vaidehyām eva | kṣatrāyāṃ śūdrajaniṣādād āhiṇḍakaḥ | ayaṃ veṇujīvī śvapākaḥ |
caṇḍālena tu sopāko mūlavyasanavṛttimān |
pulkasyāṃ jāyate pāpaḥ sadā sajjanagarhitaḥ ||
mūlaṃ mukhyaṃ [292] vyasanam asuviyogo vadhyamāraṇaṃ saiva vṛttiḥ | kṣatrāyāṃ śūdrajo niṣādaḥ tasmāc chūdrāyāṃ pulkasī | ayaṃ kasāra iti prasiddhaḥ |
niṣādastrī tu caṇḍālāt putram antyāvasāyinam |
smaśānagocaraṃ sūte bāhyānām api garhitam ||
kecid ādipurāṇe ‘py uktāḥ |
niṣādyām atha śūdreṇa kukkuṭaḥ syāt karoti saḥ |
cakratomaraśūlāsilakuṭaprāsatomarān ||
cakrāṇi śastrajālāni bhiṇḍipālāṃś ca bhūbhujām |
kukkuṭānāṃ prahartā ca rājakukkuṭakhelanaḥ ||
ambaṣṭhyāṃ jāyate veno vaidehena karoti saḥ |
paṭahaṃ vādayan sphītaṃ rājādeśaṃ prajāsv api ||
pulkasaḥ kukkuṭo venas traya āraṇyavāsinaḥ |
āyogavyāṃ dasyunā ca sairandhro nāma jāyate ||
dasyuḥ pauṇḍrakādiḥ prāg uktaḥ |
mṛgādivadhajīvī ca pāśabandhena nityaśaḥ |
rājastrīṇāṃ sūtikānāṃ dvārarakṣāṃ karoti saḥ ||
āyogavyāṃ niṣādāt tu dhīvaro nāma jāyate |
nāvo nadīnāṃ kartā sa tāṃ ca saṃvāhayet sadā ||
niṣādād atha vaidehyāṃ kārāvāraḥ karoti saḥ |
carmaṇāṃ chedanaṃ nityāṃ sīvanaṃ krayavikrayam ||
niṣādyām atha vaidehāj jāyate candramecakaḥ |
āraṇyānāṃ paśūnāṃ sa karoti ca vadhaṃ sadā ||
tasya strīgamanāt kṣattā śvapākaṃ janayet sutam |
śvamāṃsādam araṇyasthaṃ pāpaśūrpopajīvanam ||
caṇḍālād api vaihyāṃ pāṇḍusopākasaṃjñakaḥ |
upānaccarmaveṇūnāṃ vikrayaṃ hi karoti saḥ ||
āhiṇḍako niṣādyāṃ ca vaidehād api jāyate |
yo dasyūnāṃ tu vadhyānāṃ parirakṣāṃ karoti ca ||
antyāvasāyī cāṇḍalān niṣādyāṃ jāyate naraḥ |
smaśānavāsi tadvṛttiś caṇḍāleṣv api garhitaḥ ||
anāthaṃ nagarc chāvaṃ dūraṃ te ca nayanty api |
śayyābharaṇavastrāṇi vadhyānāṃ rājaśāsanāt ||
nirdiṣtaṃ vadhadaṇḍaṃ tu kṛtvā gṛhṇanti nityaśaḥ |
vivāhādi prakurvanti samāne varṇasaṃkare ||
mṛtā gobrāhmaṇārthaṃ tu kadācit saṃtaranti te |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
naujīvikā ca niṣādānām | kaivarttānāṃ mṛgapaśupakṣighāto māṃsavikrayaś ca | āvṛtānāṃ hastyaśvādhikāraḥ | ābhīrāṇāṃ go’jāvirakṣā | dhigvaṇānāṃ carmakaraṇaṃ tadvikrayajīvanaṃ ca | kārāvarāṇāṃ carmacchedanaṃ dīpāsanarakṣaṇaṃ chatradhāraṇaṃ narāṇāṃ vāhanaṃ ca |
ayaṃ kahāraḥ bhoī iti prasiddhaḥ |
tantuvāyatvavṛkṣachedanaropaṇāni kukkuṭānām | andhramedānāṃ gṛhadvārāśucisthānāvaskaraśodhanam guhyāṅgasparśanocchiṣṭaviṇmūtrohanaṃ cakravṛttitā ca guhyāṅgasparśaḥ śaucaṃ adhonāpitatvaṃ vā |
cakravṛttitā tailakatvam iti smṛtimañjarī |
madhūkānāṃ prātar nṛpatisaṃstavākhyānavarṇanaṃ nidrābodhanam | pravrajyāvasitānāṃ yāvajjīvaṃ dāsavṛttitvam | mūlavṛttitvaṃ śūrpakaraṇaṃ ca pulkasānām | mūlaṃ vṛkṣauṣadhyādeḥ | caṇḍālāntyāvasāyināṃ vanasmaśānarakṣaṇaṃ garāgniviṣaprayogaś ca | strīṇāṃ bhartṛśuśrūṣā | navyañjanapānasaṃskāro nṛtyagītakāmasūtrādhyayanaṃ ca | yat puṃsām annavyañjanādi kāryaṃ tat tat strīṇām api cāraṇādistrīṇāṃ tu nṛtyādi | sarve bāhyāḥ sarvadharmabahiṣkṛtāḥ |
anyatra prāṇisaṃrakṣaṇāt ahiṃsādiś ca dharmaḥ sarvatulyaḥ |
ahiṃsā satyam asteyaṃ śaucam indriyanigrahaḥ |
dānaṃ damo dayā śāntiḥ sarveṣāṃ dharmasādhanam |
iti yājñavalkyokteḥ | pitror ābdike sajātibhojanaṃ vastrahemādidānaṃ kāryam ity uktaṃ prāk |
[298]
śaṅkhas tv anyathāha |
sūtānām [98]aṅgasaṃskārakriyākeśaśmaśruromanakhavāpanasnāna-vilepanālaṃkaraṇāśvadamanayogyāśanapoṣaṇāni karmāṇi |
yogyāśanaṃ ghāsādi |
kṣattuḥ pratihārarakṣādautye | māgadhānāṃ mṛdaṅgavīṇāveṇuvādananṛtyagīta-siddhikopasthānagāthālokapratyūṣaprabodhanāni karmāṇi |
siddhir iti kopasamaye tacchāmakagāthāṃ paṭhet | japajīvetiśabdaḥ siddhiśabdārthaḥ |
ambaṣṭhānāṃ kāyaśalyaśalākāvimocanonmādaklībajvarāpasmāracikitsāpoṣaṇāni karmāṇi |
kāyāc chalyasya śalākāḥ śirāḥ tāsāṃ mocanaṃ bhedaḥ |
vaidehakānāṃ vastrachedanasīvanarañjnāvanirṇejanavāpītaḍāgodaka-pānārāmāvakaraśodhanāni karmāṇi | ugrāṇāṃ kuñjaragrahaṇarājachatra-dhāraṇapurapradhāraṇabandhanāgārasaṃyamanāni | venānāṃ laṅghanaplavanākrīḍāniyuddhamāyāśambarendrajālāni karmāṇi | niyuddhaṃ bā
niyuddhaṃ bāhuyuddham | māyāśambaraṃ lāghavād dravyaparivartanam | indrajālam asadvastudarśanādi |
kukkuṭānāṃ praharaṇacakratomarabhiṇḍipālaprāsāsiparaśulaguḍalohajālikā-saṃdhānāni |
praharaṇam āyudham | lohajālikā kavacam |
āyogavānām ayaskaraṇatakṣaṇakumbhakaraṇāni suvarṇarajatalohāsicarmavṛttitā ceti | pulkasānāṃ nagaravīthīsaṃrakṣaṇajāgaraṇānāthamṛtanirharaṇālekhyapatra-cchedanāni madhupātanaṃ mākṣikoddharaṇaṃ bilakartanaṃ kūpādikhananaṃ ca | cāṇḍālānāṃ smaśānasaṃrakṣaṇaśūlaparivāpanavadhyaghātāni karmāṇi | śvapacānāṃ caitat |
atraikasya nānākarmaṇāṃ vikalpaḥ samuccayo vā | anye jātiviveke jñeyā iti saṃkṣepaḥ |
nanu kalau kṣatriyavaiśyābhāva ukto bhāgavate navamaskandhe |
ikṣvākūṇām ayaṃ vaṃśaḥ sumitrānto bhaviṣyati |
yatas taṃ prāpya rājānaṃ saṃsthāṃ prāpsyati vai kalau ||
devāpir yogam āsthāya kalāpagrāmam āśritaḥ |
somavaṃśe kalau naṣṭe kṛtādau sthāpayiṣyati || iti |
dvādaśe ‘pi |
eteṣāṃ nāmaliṅgānāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ mahātmnām |
kathāmātrāvaśiṣṭānāṃ kīrtir eva sthitā bhuvi ||
devāpiḥ śaṃtanor bhrātā maruś cekṣvākuvaṃśajaḥ |
kalāpagrāma āsāte mahāyogabalānvitau ||
tāv ihaitya kaler ante vāsudevānaśikṣitau |
varṇāśramayutaṃ dharmaṃ pūrvavat prathayiṣyataḥ ||
viṣṇupurāṇe ‘pi |
devāpiḥ pauravo rājā maruś cekṣvākuvaṃsajaḥ |
kṛte yuga ihāgatya kṣatraprāvartakau hi tau ||
tathā |
mahāpadmapatiḥ kaścin nandaḥ kṣatravināśakṛt |
nandaś ca kaler ādau parikṣito ‘nantaraṃ jātaḥ ||
ārambhya bhavato janma yāvan nandābhiṣecanam |
etad varṣasahasraṃ tu śataṃ pañcadaśottaram ||
purāṇāntare ‘pi |
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyā vaiśyāḥ śūdrā varṇās trayo dvijāḥ |
yuge yuge sthitāḥ sarve kalāv ādyantayoḥ sthitiḥ | iti |
ataḥ kathaṃ dvijasaṃkarajāḥ uktāḥ | maivam |
kalau tu bījabhūtās tu kecit tiṣṭhanti bhūtale |
tathaiva devāpipurū sāṃprathaṃ samavasthitau ||
iti viṣṇupurāṇāt,
brahmakṣatraviśaḥ śūdrā bījārthe ya iha sthitāḥ |
kṛte yuge hi taiḥ sārdhaṃ nirviśeṣās tadābhavan ||
iti mātyokteś ca, pracchannarūpāḥ svakarmabhraṣṭāḥ kṣatriyā vaiśyāś ca santy eva kvacid ity asmatpitṛcaraṇāḥ |
yo bhāṭṭatantragahanārṇavakarṇadhāraḥ
śāstrāntareṣu nikhileṣv api marmabhettā |
yo ‘tra śramaḥ kila kṛtaḥ kamalākareṇa
prīto ‘munāstu sukṛtī budharāmakṛṣṇaḥ ||
iti śrīmajjagadgurumīmāṃsakanārāyaṇabhaṭṭasūrisūnu-
rāmakṛṣṇabhaṭṭātmajadinakarabhaṭṭānujakamalākarakṛte
śūdradharmatattve śūdrasamasaṃkarajasvarūpaṃ
tadvṛttinirūpaṇaṃ ca samāptam |
samāptaś cāyaṃ nibandhaḥ |
